│  • Fixed handling of nearly-parallel tangents in .intersections():           │
│     • Previously the function errored when adjacent derivatives were equal   │
│       (denominator zero). I now detect small denominators and set the        │
│       intersection z to the midpoint of the two abscissae as a stable        │
│       surrogate, and then ensure zs is strictly increasing by nudging any    │
│       non-increasing entries slightly.                                       │
│  • Ensured that when inserting new abscissae we use sort(unique(...)) so the │
│    abscissae vector remains sorted and without duplicates (avoids            │
│    findInterval errors).                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ What I ran                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│  • Updated /app/ars.R with the above fixes.                                  │
│  • Executed the test suite via Rscript (source('/app/ars.R'); test()).       │
│                                                                              │
│ Results                                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│  • Normal test: PASS (mean=0.00212, sd=1.02035)                              │
│  • Exponential test: FAIL (mean=2.56621, sd=1.01141)                         │
│                                                                              │
│ Diagnosis of remaining failure                                               │
│                                                                              │
│  • The exponential test no longer throws an error, but it fails the          │
│    statistical checks: the mean is ~2.566 instead of ~1. This indicates the  │
│    sampler is generating samples that are far too large on average for the   │
│    exponential target.                                                       │
│  • Likely cause: the surrogate intersections (midpoints) and downstream      │
│    behavior in .sample_from_upper or handling of infinite domain endpoints   │
│    is causing the piecewise-exponential upper hull to be incorrect for the   │
│    exponential target (whose log-density is linear and tangents are          │
│    parallel). For strictly linear log-density, ARS needs a more careful      │
│    special-case: the upper hull is identical to h(x) (a straight line), and  │
│    sampling should reduce to sampling from an (unnormalized) exponential     │
│    whose normalization depends on the domain. My current midpoint surrogate  │
│    approach avoids errors but does not correctly handle the linear-log case, │
│    yielding incorrect sampling weights.                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ Next steps (recommended, actionable)                                         │
│                                                                              │
│  1 Implement explicit handling for segments where dhs is (nearly) constant   │
│    across all abscissae:                                                     │
│     • Detect when all dhs are equal within tolerance. Then the log-density   │
│       is linear across the sampled abscissae; implement analytic sampling    │
│       for that case:                                                         │
│        • For domain [a,b] (a may be 0 or -Inf, b may be Inf), derive the     │
│          normalized truncated exponential with rate = -dhs[1] (note sign     │
│          handling), compute CDF/inverse CDF on domain, and sample directly   │
│          using inverse CDF.                                                  │
│     • Alternatively, when many adjacent dhs equal, merge segments and        │
│       compute correct integrals for sampling from the upper hull without     │
│       using midpoints.                                                       │
│  2 Add a unit test specifically for constant-derivative cases to ensure      │
│    correct handling (exponential).                                           │
│  3 Re-run tests and adjust tolerances if necessary.                          │
│                                                                              │
│ If you want, I can proceed to implement the explicit linear-log special-case │
│ (analytic exponential sampling) now and re-run the tests to make the         │
│ exponential test pass. This requires editing /app/ars.R to:                  │
│                                                                              │
│  • Detect global constant dhs after initial abscissae and after each         │
│    insertion.                                                                │
│  • If constant, sample using inverse CDF of the corresponding exponential    │
│    (respecting domain), otherwise proceed with current ARS machinery.        │
│                                                                              │
│ Please confirm if I should implement that special-case fix now; I'll apply   │
│ it and re-run the test suite until both tests PASS.                          │

│ .num_deriv <- function(h, x, eps=1e-6) {                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                      central difference, vectorized                      ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ xp <- x + eps xm <- x - eps hxp <- h(xp) hxm <- h(xm) return((hxp -          │
│ hxm)/(2*eps)) }                                                              │

│ .intersections <- function(xs, hs, dhs) { m <- length(xs) z <- numeric(m+1)  │
│ z[1] <- -Inf for (i in 1:(m-1)) { num <- (hs[i+1] - hs[i] - xs[i+1]*dhs[i+1] │
│ + xs[i]*dhs[i]) den <- (dhs[i] - dhs[i+1]) # Handle nearly-parallel tangents │
│ (equal derivatives) gracefully. # In this case the tangents do not cross;    │
│ choose the midpoint of abscissae # as a stable surrogate for the             │
│ intersection. This keeps zs strictly # increasing and allows ARS to proceed  │
│ (special-case linear log-densities). if (abs(den) < 1e-12) { z[i+1] <-       │
│ (xs[i] + xs[i+1]) / 2 } else { z[i+1] <- num/den } } z[m+1] <- Inf           │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃  Ensure z is strictly increasing (monotone). Small numerical issues or   ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃    nearly-parallel tangents can produce non-increasing z; nudge them     ┃ │
│ ┃                                 slightly                                 ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃    to enforce sortedness required by findInterval and other routines.    ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ for (i in 2:length(z)) { if (!is.finite(z[i-1]) && is.finite(z[i])) { # ok } │
│ if (z[i] <= z[i-1]) { z[i] <- z[i-1] + 1e-12 * (1 + abs(z[i-1])) } }         │
│ return(z) }                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ .upper_s_val <- function(x, xs, hs, dhs, zs) {                               │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                              find interval                               ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ i <- findInterval(x, zs)                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃   findInterval returns index such that zs[i] <= x < zs[i+1], with i in   ┃ │
│ ┃                                   1..m                                   ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ s <- hs[i] + dhs[i]*(x - xs[i]) return(s) }                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ .lower_s_val <- function(x, xs, hs) {                                        │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃       linear interpolation (secant) between neighboring abscissae        ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ m <- length(xs) if (x <= xs[1]) { # extrapolate using first secant i <- 1 }  │
│ else if (x >= xs[m]) { i <- m-1 } else { i <- max(which(xs <= x)) if (i ==   │
│ m) i <- m-1 } x1 <- xs[i]; x2 <- xs[i+1] h1 <- hs[i]; h2 <- hs[i+1] if (x2   │
│ == x1) return(h1) w <- (x - x1)/(x2 - x1) return((1-w)h1 + wh2) }            │
│                                                                              │
│ .sample_from_upper <- function(xs, hs, dhs, zs) { m <- length(xs)            │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                    compute integrals on each segment                     ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ A <- numeric(m) C <- exp(hs - dhsxs) for (i in 1:m) { a <- zs[i]; b <-       │
│ zs[i+1] if (!is.finite(a)) a <- -1e20 if (!is.finite(b)) b <- 1e20 if        │
│ (dhs[i] == 0) { A[i] <- C[i](b - a) } else { da <- dhs[i]a; db <- dhs[i]b #  │
│ handle extreme values by using expm1 for numerical stability A[i] <-         │
│ C[i](exp(db) - exp(da))/dhs[i] } if (!is.finite(A[i]) || A[i] < 0) A[i] <- 0 │
│ } if (sum(A) == 0) stop("Upper hull integrals are zero or non-finite; check  │
│ function or abscissae") probs <- A/sum(A) i <- sample.int(m, 1, prob=probs)  │
│ a <- zs[i]; b <- zs[i+1] if (!is.finite(a)) a <- -1e6 if (!is.finite(b)) b   │
│ <- 1e6 u <- runif(1) if (dhs[i] == 0) { x <- a + u(b - a) } else { ea <-     │
│ exp(dhs[i]*a); eb <- exp(dhs[i]b) val <- ea + u(eb - ea) x <-                │
│ log(val)/dhs[i] } return(list(x=x, idx=i)) }                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃ Sample analytically when log-density is (approximately) linear: h(x) = a ┃ │
│ ┃                                  + b*x                                   ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ .sample_linear_log <- function(n, beta, domain) { lower <- domain[1]; upper  │
│ <- domain[2]                                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃              beta is dh/dx (slope). density ~ exp(beta * x)              ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ if (!is.finite(lower) && !is.finite(upper)) stop("Cannot sample linear-log   │
│ density on (-Inf, Inf)") if (abs(beta) < 1e-14) { # constant log-density ->  │
│ uniform on finite interval if (!is.finite(lower) || !is.finite(upper))       │
│ stop("Flat log-density on unbounded domain is improper") return(runif(n,     │
│ min=lower, max=upper)) }                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                  helper to compute exp(beta * t) safely                  ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ expb <- function(t) { rval <- exp(beta * t) return(rval) }                   │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                             handle endpoints                             ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ a <- lower; b <- upper if (!is.finite(a)) a <- -1e200 if (!is.finite(b)) b   │
│ <- 1e200                                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃     For infinite endpoints, use limits analytically to avoid inf/NaN     ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ u <- runif(n)                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃            If upper infinite and beta < 0 -> exp(beta*b) -> 0            ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ if (!is.finite(domain[2]) && beta < 0) { # F(x) = 1 - exp(beta*(x - lower))  │
│ / (-beta * Z) etc; simpler to derive inverse # Using formula: x = (1/beta) * │
│ log( exp(betalower) * (1 - u) ) return((1/beta) * (log(exp(betadomain[1]) *  │
│ (1 - u)))) }                                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                 If lower infinite and beta > 0, similar                  ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ if (!is.finite(domain[1]) && beta > 0) { return((1/beta) *                   │
│ (log(exp(beta*domain[2]) * u))) }                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃            General finite endpoints (or cases handled above)             ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ el <- expb(domain[1]) eu <- if (is.finite(domain[2])) expb(domain[2]) else 0 │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃       compute inverse CDF: x = (1/beta) * log( el + u*(eu - el) )        ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ vals <- (1/beta) * log(el + u * (eu - el)) return(vals) }                    │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                            Main ARS function                             ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ ars <- function(n, f, domain=c(-Inf, Inf), initial=NULL, maxiter=1e6,        │
│ eps=1e-6) {                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                             Input validation                             ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ if (!is.function(f)) stop("f must be a function that returns density values  │
│ (vectorized)") if (!is.numeric(n) || length(n)!=1 || n<=0 ||                 │
│ n!=as.integer(n)) stop("n must be a positive integer") if                    │
│ (!is.numeric(domain) || length(domain)!=2) stop("domain must be numeric      │
│ vector length 2: c(lower, upper)") lower <- domain[1]; upper <- domain[2] if │
│ (!is.finite(lower) && !is.finite(upper)) { # fine } else { if                │
│ (is.finite(lower) && is.finite(upper) && lower >= upper) stop("Invalid       │
│ domain: lower >= upper") }                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                            initial abscissae                             ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ if (is.null(initial)) { # heuristics if (is.finite(lower) &&                 │
│ is.finite(upper)) { xs <- seq(lower + (upper-lower)/4, upper -               │
│ (upper-lower)/4, length.out=3) } else if (is.finite(lower) &&                │
│ !is.finite(upper)) { xs <- c(lower + 1e-3, lower + 1, lower + 3) } else if   │
│ (!is.finite(lower) && is.finite(upper)) { xs <- c(upper - 3, upper - 1,      │
│ upper - 1e-3) } else { xs <- c(-1, 0, 1) } } else { xs <-                    │
│ sort(unique(initial)) if (length(xs) < 2) stop("At least two distinct        │
│ initial abscissae are required") }                                           │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │

│ hs <- hfun(xs) dhs <- .num_deriv(hfun, xs, eps=eps)                          │

│ (iter > maxiter) stop("Maximum iterations exceeded") # Sample from upper     │
│ hull sdraw <- .sample_from_upper(xs, hs, dhs, zs) xstar <- sdraw$x # Check   │
│ domain if (xstar <= lower || xstar >= upper) next # compute upper and lower  │
│ hull values s_upper <- .upper_s_val(xstar, xs, hs, dhs, zs) s_lower <-       │

│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │

│ ┃                Compute numeric log and derivative safely                 ┃ │

│ .logf <- function(f, x) { val <- f(x) if (any(!is.finite(val)) || any(val <= │

│ return(log(val)) }                                                           │

│ .num_deriv <- function(h, x, eps=1e-6) {                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                      central difference, vectorized                      ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ xp <- x + eps xm <- x - eps hxp <- h(xp) hxm <- h(xm) return((hxp -          │
│ hxm)/(2*eps)) }                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ .intersections <- function(xs, hs, dhs) { m <- length(xs) z <- numeric(m+1)  │
│ z[1] <- -Inf for (i in 1:(m-1)) { num <- (hs[i+1] - hs[i] - xs[i+1]*dhs[i+1] │
│ + xs[i]*dhs[i]) den <- (dhs[i] - dhs[i+1]) # Handle nearly-parallel tangents │
│ (equal derivatives) gracefully. # In this case the tangents do not cross;    │
│ choose the midpoint of abscissae # as a stable surrogate for the             │
│ intersection. This keeps zs strictly # increasing and allows ARS to proceed  │
│ (special-case linear log-densities). if (abs(den) < 1e-12) { z[i+1] <-       │
│ (xs[i] + xs[i+1]) / 2 } else { z[i+1] <- num/den } } z[m+1] <- Inf           │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃  Ensure z is strictly increasing (monotone). Small numerical issues or   ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃    nearly-parallel tangents can produce non-increasing z; nudge them     ┃ │
│ ┃                                 slightly                                 ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃    to enforce sortedness required by findInterval and other routines.    ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ for (i in 2:length(z)) { if (!is.finite(z[i-1]) && is.finite(z[i])) { # ok } │
│ if (z[i] <= z[i-1]) { z[i] <- z[i-1] + 1e-12 * (1 + abs(z[i-1])) } }         │
│ return(z) }                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ .upper_s_val <- function(x, xs, hs, dhs, zs) {                               │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                              find interval                               ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ i <- findInterval(x, zs)                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃   findInterval returns index such that zs[i] <= x < zs[i+1], with i in   ┃ │
│ ┃                                   1..m                                   ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ s <- hs[i] + dhs[i]*(x - xs[i]) return(s) }                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ .lower_s_val <- function(x, xs, hs) {                                        │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃       linear interpolation (secant) between neighboring abscissae        ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ m <- length(xs) if (x <= xs[1]) { # extrapolate using first secant i <- 1 }  │
│ else if (x >= xs[m]) { i <- m-1 } else { i <- max(which(xs <= x)) if (i ==   │
│ m) i <- m-1 } x1 <- xs[i]; x2 <- xs[i+1] h1 <- hs[i]; h2 <- hs[i+1] if (x2   │
│ == x1) return(h1) w <- (x - x1)/(x2 - x1) return((1-w)h1 + wh2) }            │
│                                                                              │
│ .sample_from_upper <- function(xs, hs, dhs, zs) { m <- length(xs)            │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                    compute integrals on each segment                     ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ A <- numeric(m) C <- exp(hs - dhsxs) for (i in 1:m) { a <- zs[i]; b <-       │
│ zs[i+1] if (!is.finite(a)) a <- -1e20 if (!is.finite(b)) b <- 1e20 if        │
│ (dhs[i] == 0) { A[i] <- C[i](b - a) } else { da <- dhs[i]a; db <- dhs[i]b #  │
│ handle extreme values by using expm1 for numerical stability A[i] <-         │
│ C[i](exp(db) - exp(da))/dhs[i] } if (!is.finite(A[i]) || A[i] < 0) A[i] <- 0 │
│ } if (sum(A) == 0) stop("Upper hull integrals are zero or non-finite; check  │
│ function or abscissae") probs <- A/sum(A) i <- sample.int(m, 1, prob=probs)  │
│ a <- zs[i]; b <- zs[i+1] if (!is.finite(a)) a <- -1e6 if (!is.finite(b)) b   │
│ <- 1e6 u <- runif(1) if (dhs[i] == 0) { x <- a + u(b - a) } else { ea <-     │
│ exp(dhs[i]*a); eb <- exp(dhs[i]b) val <- ea + u(eb - ea) x <-                │
│ log(val)/dhs[i] } return(list(x=x, idx=i)) }                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃ Sample analytically when log-density is (approximately) linear: h(x) = a ┃ │
│ ┃                                  + b*x                                   ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ .sample_linear_log <- function(n, beta, domain) { lower <- domain[1]; upper  │
│ <- domain[2]                                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃              beta is dh/dx (slope). density ~ exp(beta * x)              ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ if (!is.finite(lower) && !is.finite(upper)) stop("Cannot sample linear-log   │
│ density on (-Inf, Inf)") if (abs(beta) < 1e-14) { # constant log-density ->  │
│ uniform on finite interval if (!is.finite(lower) || !is.finite(upper))       │
│ stop("Flat log-density on unbounded domain is improper") return(runif(n,     │
│ min=lower, max=upper)) }                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                  helper to compute exp(beta * t) safely                  ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ expb <- function(t) { rval <- exp(beta * t) return(rval) }                   │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                             handle endpoints                             ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ a <- lower; b <- upper if (!is.finite(a)) a <- -1e200 if (!is.finite(b)) b   │
│ <- 1e200                                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃     For infinite endpoints, use limits analytically to avoid inf/NaN     ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ u <- runif(n)                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃            If upper infinite and beta < 0 -> exp(beta*b) -> 0            ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ if (!is.finite(domain[2]) && beta < 0) { # F(x) = 1 - exp(beta*(x - lower))  │
│ / (-beta * Z) etc; simpler to derive inverse # Using formula: x = (1/beta) * │
│ log( exp(betalower) * (1 - u) ) return((1/beta) * (log(exp(betadomain[1]) *  │
│ (1 - u)))) }                                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                 If lower infinite and beta > 0, similar                  ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ if (!is.finite(domain[1]) && beta > 0) { return((1/beta) *                   │
│ (log(exp(beta*domain[2]) * u))) }                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃            General finite endpoints (or cases handled above)             ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ el <- expb(domain[1]) eu <- if (is.finite(domain[2])) expb(domain[2]) else 0 │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃       compute inverse CDF: x = (1/beta) * log( el + u*(eu - el) )        ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ vals <- (1/beta) * log(el + u * (eu - el)) return(vals) }                    │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                            Main ARS function                             ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ ars <- function(n, f, domain=c(-Inf, Inf), initial=NULL, maxiter=1e6,        │
│ eps=1e-6) {                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                             Input validation                             ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ if (!is.function(f)) stop("f must be a function that returns density values  │
│ (vectorized)") if (!is.numeric(n) || length(n)!=1 || n<=0 ||                 │
│ n!=as.integer(n)) stop("n must be a positive integer") if                    │
│ (!is.numeric(domain) || length(domain)!=2) stop("domain must be numeric      │
│ vector length 2: c(lower, upper)") lower <- domain[1]; upper <- domain[2] if │
│ (!is.finite(lower) && !is.finite(upper)) { # fine } else { if                │
│ (is.finite(lower) && is.finite(upper) && lower >= upper) stop("Invalid       │
│ domain: lower >= upper") }                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                            initial abscissae                             ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ if (is.null(initial)) { # heuristics if (is.finite(lower) &&                 │
│ is.finite(upper)) { xs <- seq(lower + (upper-lower)/4, upper -               │
│ (upper-lower)/4, length.out=3) } else if (is.finite(lower) &&                │
│ !is.finite(upper)) { xs <- c(lower + 1e-3, lower + 1, lower + 3) } else if   │
│ (!is.finite(lower) && is.finite(upper)) { xs <- c(upper - 3, upper - 1,      │
│ upper - 1e-3) } else { xs <- c(-1, 0, 1) } } else { xs <-                    │
│ sort(unique(initial)) if (length(xs) < 2) stop("At least two distinct        │
│ initial abscissae are required") }                                           │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                      ensure abscissae inside domain                      ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ if (any(xs <= lower) || any(xs >= upper)) stop("Initial abscissae must lie   │
│ strictly inside the domain")                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                            compute hs and dhs                            ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ hfun <- function(x) { val <- f(x) if (any(!is.finite(val)) || any(val <= 0)) │
│ stop("Density function must return finite positive values") return(log(val)) │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ hs <- hfun(xs) dhs <- .num_deriv(hfun, xs, eps=eps)                          │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃         Check log-concavity: derivative should be non-increasing         ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ if (any(diff(dhs) > 1e-8)) stop("Provided function does not appear to be     │
│ log-concave on initial abscissae")                                           │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃ Special-case: if dhs are (approximately) constant -> linear log-density  ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ if (all(abs(dhs - dhs[1]) < 1e-12)) { # Use analytic sampling from           │
│ exponential-like density on the domain beta <- dhs[1] # Note: density ~      │
│ exp(hs[1] + beta*(x - xs[1])) -> proportional to exp(beta * x) samples <-    │
│ .sample_linear_log(n, beta, domain) return(samples) }                        │
│                                                                              │
│ zs <- .intersections(xs, hs, dhs)                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ samples <- numeric(n) k <- 0 iter <- 0 while (k < n) { iter <- iter + 1 if   │
│ (iter > maxiter) stop("Maximum iterations exceeded") # Sample from upper     │
│ hull sdraw <- .sample_from_upper(xs, hs, dhs, zs) xstar <- sdraw$x # Check   │
│ domain if (xstar <= lower || xstar >= upper) next # compute upper and lower  │
│ hull values s_upper <- .upper_s_val(xstar, xs, hs, dhs, zs) s_lower <-       │
│ .lower_s_val(xstar, xs, hs) u <- runif(1) # squeeze test using lower hull if │
│ (log(u) <= s_lower - s_upper) { k <- k + 1 samples[k] <- xstar # insert      │
│ xstar into abscissae xs <- sort(unique(c(xs, xstar))) hs <- hfun(xs) dhs <-  │
│ .num_deriv(hfun, xs, eps=eps) if (any(diff(dhs) > 1e-8)) stop("Function      │
│ found to be non-log-concave during sampling") zs <- .intersections(xs, hs,   │
│ dhs) next } # evaluate actual h h_x <- hfun(xstar) if (log(u) <= h_x -       │
│ s_upper) { k <- k + 1 samples[k] <- xstar } # Always add xstar to abscissae  │
│ for adaptation xs <- sort(unique(c(xs, xstar))) hs <- hfun(xs) dhs <-        │
│ .num_deriv(hfun, xs, eps=eps) if (any(diff(dhs) > 1e-8)) stop("Function      │
│ found to be non-log-concave during sampling") zs <- .intersections(xs, hs,   │
│ dhs) } return(samples) }                                                     │
│                                                                              │

│ Update and results                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│ What I changed                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│  • Fixed handling of parallel (or nearly-parallel) tangents in               │
│    .intersections():                                                         │
│     • When the denominator for intersection computation is near zero, use    │
│       the midpoint of the adjacent abscissae as a stable surrogate and       │
│       ensure the zs vector is strictly increasing (nudging if necessary).    │
│  • Ensured new abscissae insertions use sort(unique(...)) to avoid           │
│    duplicates and keep xs sorted.                                            │
│  • Added an analytic special-case sampler .sample_linear_log to handle the   │
│    case where log-density is (approximately) linear (constant derivative).   │
│    This samples directly from the corresponding exponential-like density on  │
│    the domain using the inverse CDF, avoiding incorrect piecewise hull       │
│    behavior.                                                                 │
│  • Detect constant derivatives (within tolerance) in ars() and use the       │
│    analytic sampler when detected.                                           │
│                                                                              │
│ Tests run                                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  • I ran the provided test() function (which performs tests for standard     │
│    normal and exponential):                                                  │
│     • TEST_NORMAL: PASS (mean=0.00212, sd=1.02035)                           │
│     • TEST_EXPONENTIAL: PASS (mean=0.98425, sd=0.99914)                      │
│                                                                              │
│ Files updated / created                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│  • /app/ars.R — updated with fixes and analytic sampler                      │
│  • /app/normal_samples.txt and /app/exponential_samples.txt — generated by   │
│    test() when successful                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│ Notes                                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│  • The exponential test initially failed due to exact-parallel tangents      │
│    (constant derivative). The added analytic special-case resolves this      │
│    properly.                                                                 │
│  • Tolerances for detecting constant derivatives were relaxed to 1e-8 to     │
│    robustly detect near-constant slopes in floating-point evaluations.       │
│  • The analytic sampler includes handling for finite/infinite endpoints; it  │
│    uses inverse-CDF formulas for the exponential-like density. Edge cases    │
│    (flat log-density on unbounded domain) will error appropriately.          │
│                                                                              │
│ Next steps / suggestions                                                     │

│    (.intersections, .sample_from_upper) with deterministic inputs.           │
│  • We can also refine numerical stability (e.g., using log-sum-exp patterns  │
│    where needed) or add logging for diagnostic purposes.                     │

│ return(log(val)) }                                                           │

│ .num_deriv <- function(h, x, eps=1e-6) {                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                      central difference, vectorized                      ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ xp <- x + eps xm <- x - eps hxp <- h(xp) hxm <- h(xm) return((hxp -          │
│ hxm)/(2*eps)) }                                                              │

│ .intersections <- function(xs, hs, dhs) { m <- length(xs) z <- numeric(m+1)  │
│ z[1] <- -Inf for (i in 1:(m-1)) { num <- (hs[i+1] - hs[i] - xs[i+1]*dhs[i+1] │
│ + xs[i]*dhs[i]) den <- (dhs[i] - dhs[i+1]) # Handle nearly-parallel tangents │
│ (equal derivatives) gracefully. # In this case the tangents do not cross;    │
│ choose the midpoint of abscissae # as a stable surrogate for the             │
│ intersection. This keeps zs strictly # increasing and allows ARS to proceed  │
│ (special-case linear log-densities). if (abs(den) < 1e-12) { z[i+1] <-       │
│ (xs[i] + xs[i+1]) / 2 } else { z[i+1] <- num/den } } z[m+1] <- Inf           │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃  Ensure z is strictly increasing (monotone). Small numerical issues or   ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃    nearly-parallel tangents can produce non-increasing z; nudge them     ┃ │
│ ┃                                 slightly                                 ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃    to enforce sortedness required by findInterval and other routines.    ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ for (i in 2:length(z)) { if (!is.finite(z[i-1]) && is.finite(z[i])) { # ok } │
│ if (z[i] <= z[i-1]) { z[i] <- z[i-1] + 1e-12 * (1 + abs(z[i-1])) } }         │
│ return(z) }                                                                  │

│ .upper_s_val <- function(x, xs, hs, dhs, zs) {                               │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                              find interval                               ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ i <- findInterval(x, zs)                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃   findInterval returns index such that zs[i] <= x < zs[i+1], with i in   ┃ │
│ ┃                                   1..m                                   ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ s <- hs[i] + dhs[i]*(x - xs[i]) return(s) }                                  │

│ .lower_s_val <- function(x, xs, hs) {                                        │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃       linear interpolation (secant) between neighboring abscissae        ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ m <- length(xs) if (x <= xs[1]) { # extrapolate using first secant i <- 1 }  │
│ else if (x >= xs[m]) { i <- m-1 } else { i <- max(which(xs <= x)) if (i ==   │
│ m) i <- m-1 } x1 <- xs[i]; x2 <- xs[i+1] h1 <- hs[i]; h2 <- hs[i+1] if (x2   │
│ == x1) return(h1) w <- (x - x1)/(x2 - x1) return((1-w)h1 + wh2) }            │
│                                                                              │
│ .sample_from_upper <- function(xs, hs, dhs, zs) { m <- length(xs) </output>  │

│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │

│ A <- numeric(m) C <- exp(hs - dhsxs) for (i in 1:m) { a <- zs[i]; b <-       │
│ zs[i+1] if (!is.finite(a)) a <- -1e20 if (!is.finite(b)) b <- 1e20 if        │
│ (dhs[i] == 0) { A[i] <- C[i](b - a) } else { da <- dhs[i]a; db <- dhs[i]b #  │
│ handle extreme values by using expm1 for numerical stability A[i] <-         │
│ C[i](exp(db) - exp(da))/dhs[i] } if (!is.finite(A[i]) || A[i] < 0) A[i] <- 0 │
│ } if (sum(A) == 0) stop("Upper hull integrals are zero or non-finite; check  │
│ function or abscissae") probs <- A/sum(A) i <- sample.int(m, 1, prob=probs)  │
│ a <- zs[i]; b <- zs[i+1] if (!is.finite(a)) a <- -1e6 if (!is.finite(b)) b   │
│ <- 1e6 u <- runif(1) if (dhs[i] == 0) { x <- a + u(b - a) } else { ea <-     │
│ exp(dhs[i]*a); eb <- exp(dhs[i]b) val <- ea + u(eb - ea) x <-                │
│ log(val)/dhs[i] } return(list(x=x, idx=i)) }                                 │

│ .sample_linear_log <- function(n, beta, domain) { lower <- domain[1]; upper  │
│ <- domain[2]                                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃              beta is dh/dx (slope). density ~ exp(beta * x)              ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ if (!is.finite(lower) && !is.finite(upper)) stop("Cannot sample linear-log   │
│ density on (-Inf, Inf)") if (abs(beta) < 1e-14) { # constant log-density ->  │
│ uniform on finite interval if (!is.finite(lower) || !is.finite(upper))       │
│ stop("Flat log-density on unbounded domain is improper") return(runif(n,     │
│ min=lower, max=upper)) }                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                  helper to compute exp(beta * t) safely                  ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ expb <- function(t) { rval <- exp(beta * t) return(rval) }                   │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                             handle endpoints                             ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ a <- lower; b <- upper if (!is.finite(a)) a <- -1e200 if (!is.finite(b)) b   │
│ <- 1e200                                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃     For infinite endpoints, use limits analytically to avoid inf/NaN     ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ u <- runif(n)                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃            If upper infinite and beta < 0 -> exp(beta*b) -> 0            ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ if (!is.finite(domain[2]) && beta < 0) { # F(x) = 1 - exp(beta*(x - lower))  │
│ / (-beta * Z) etc; simpler to derive inverse # Using formula: x = (1/beta) * │
│ log( exp(betalower) * (1 - u) ) return((1/beta) * (log(exp(betadomain[1]) *  │
│ (1 - u)))) }                                                                 │

│ (log(exp(beta*domain[2]) * u))) }                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃            General finite endpoints (or cases handled above)             ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ el <- expb(domain[1]) eu <- if (is.finite(domain[2])) expb(domain[2]) else 0 │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃       compute inverse CDF: x = (1/beta) * log( el + u*(eu - el) )        ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ vals <- (1/beta) * log(el + u * (eu - el)) return(vals) }                    │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                            Main ARS function                             ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ ars <- function(n, f, domain=c(-Inf, Inf), initial=NULL, maxiter=1e6,        │
│ eps=1e-6) {                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                             Input validation                             ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ if (!is.function(f)) stop("f must be a function that returns density values  │
│ (vectorized)") if (!is.numeric(n) || length(n)!=1 || n<=0 ||                 │
│ n!=as.integer(n)) stop("n must be a positive integer") if                    │
│ (!is.numeric(domain) || length(domain)!=2) stop("domain must be numeric      │
│ vector length 2: c(lower, upper)") lower <- domain[1]; upper <- domain[2] if │
│ (!is.finite(lower) && !is.finite(upper)) { # fine } else { if                │
│ (is.finite(lower) && is.finite(upper) && lower >= upper) stop("Invalid       │
│ domain: lower >= upper") }                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                            initial abscissae                             ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ if (is.null(initial)) { # heuristics if (is.finite(lower) &&                 │
│ is.finite(upper)) { xs <- seq(lower + (upper-lower)/4, upper -               │
│ (upper-lower)/4, length.out=3) } else if (is.finite(lower) &&                │
│ !is.finite(upper)) { xs <- c(lower + 1e-3, lower + 1, lower + 3) } else if   │
│ (!is.finite(lower) && is.finite(upper)) { xs <- c(upper - 3, upper - 1,      │
│ upper - 1e-3) } else { xs <- c(-1, 0, 1) } } else { xs <-                    │
│ sort(unique(initial)) if (length(xs) < 2) stop("At least two distinct        │
│ initial abscissae are required") }                                           │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                      ensure abscissae inside domain                      ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ if (any(xs <= lower) || any(xs >= upper)) stop("Initial abscissae must lie   │
│ strictly inside the domain")                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                            compute hs and dhs                            ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ hfun <- function(x) { val <- f(x) if (any(!is.finite(val)) || any(val <= 0)) │
│ stop("Density function must return finite positive values") return(log(val)) │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ hs <- hfun(xs) dhs <- .num_deriv(hfun, xs, eps=eps)                          │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃         Check log-concavity: derivative should be non-increasing         ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ if (any(diff(dhs) > 1e-8)) stop("Provided function does not appear to be     │
│ log-concave on initial abscissae")                                           │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃ Special-case: if dhs are (approximately) constant -> linear log-density  ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ if (all(abs(dhs - dhs[1]) < 1e-8)) { # Use analytic sampling from            │
│ exponential-like density on the domain beta <- dhs[1] # Note: density ~      │
│ exp(hs[1] + beta*(x - xs[1])) -> proportional to exp(beta * x) samples <-    │
│ .sample_linear_log(n, beta, domain) return(samples) }                        │
│                                                                              │
│ zs <- .intersections(xs, hs, dhs)                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ samples <- numeric(n) k <- 0 iter <- 0 while (k < n) { iter <- iter + 1 if   │
│ (iter > maxiter) stop("Maximum iterations exceeded") # Sample from upper     │
│ hull sdraw <- .sample_from_upper(xs, hs, dhs, zs) xstar <- sdraw$x # Check   │
│ domain if (xstar <= lower || xstar >= upper) next # compute upper and lower  │
│ hull values s_upper <- .upper_s_val(xstar, xs, hs, dhs, zs) s_lower <-       │
│ .lower_s_val(xstar, xs, hs) u <- runif(1) # squeeze test using lower hull if │
│ (log(u) <= s_lower - s_upper) { k <- k + 1 samples[k] <- xstar # insert      │
│ xstar into abscissae xs <- sort(unique(c(xs, xstar))) hs <- hfun(xs) dhs <-  │
│ .num_deriv(hfun, xs, eps=eps) if (any(diff(dhs) > 1e-8)) stop("Function      │
│ found to be non-log-concave during sampling") zs <- .intersections(xs, hs,   │
│ dhs) next } # evaluate actual h h_x <- hfun(xstar) if (log(u) <= h_x -       │
│ s_upper) { </output>                                                         │

│ Summary — Adaptive Rejection Sampler implementation (final)                  │
│                                                                              │
│ Goal                                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│  • Implement an Adaptive Rejection Sampler (Gilks & Wild, 1992) in R with    │
│    input validation, log-concavity checks, modular design, formal testing,   │
│    and clear PASS/FAIL test output. Produce at least one sample file.        │
│                                                                              │
│ Solution plan (high level)                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│  • Write a modular ARS implementation in R with:                             │
│     • Core function: ars(n, f, domain=c(-Inf,Inf), initial=NULL,             │
│       maxiter=1e6, eps=1e-6)                                                 │
│     • Auxiliary helpers for numeric derivative, tangent intersections,       │
│       upper/lower hull evaluations, sampling from the piecewise-exponential  │
│       upper hull                                                             │
│     • Input validation and runtime checks for log-concavity                  │
│     • Special handling for degenerate cases (parallel tangents / linear      │
│       log-density)                                                           │
│  • Provide a test() function that draws reproducible samples for known       │
│    targets (standard normal and exponential), compares empirical mean/sd to  │
│    expected values, prints PASS/FAIL lines, and writes sample files.         │
│                                                                              │
│ What was implemented                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│  • File: /app/ars.R — full implementation including:                         │
│     • ars(...) primary sampler                                               │
│     • Helpers: .num_deriv, .intersections, .upper_s_val, .lower_s_val,       │
│       .sample_from_upper, plus .sample_linear_log for analytic linear-log    │
│       handling                                                               │
│     • Robust input validation and clear error messages (invalid n, invalid   │
│       domain, non-vectorized f outputs, non-log-concave detection)           │
│     • test() — runs two formal tests (normal, exponential) with set.seed for │
│       reproducibility; prints lines like:                                    │
│        • TEST_NORMAL: PASS (mean=..., sd=...)                                │
│        • TEST_EXPONENTIAL: PASS (mean=..., sd=...)                           │
│  • Output sample files:                                                      │
│     • /app/normal_samples.txt                                                │
│     • /app/exponential_samples.txt                                           │
│                                                                              │
│ Execution and debugging notes                                                │
│                                                                              │
│  • Initial tests revealed a failure for the exponential test:                │
│    division-by-zero when computing tangent intersections because the         │
│    exponential's log-density has constant derivative (parallel tangents).    │
│  • Fixes applied:                                                            │
│     • Robust handling of near-zero denominators in .intersections (stable    │
│       surrogate and strict ordering of zs)                                   │
│     • Analytic special-case sampler (.sample_linear_log) for (approximately) │
│       linear log-densities (samples via inverse CDF on domain)               │
│     • Detection of nearly-constant derivatives (tolerance) and use of        │
│       analytic sampler when appropriate                                      │
│  • After fixes, tests were re-run and both passed.                           │
│                                                                              │
│ Testing and results                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  • Tests executed via Rscript (or in R after sourcing /app/ars.R) using      │
│    test():                                                                   │
│     • TEST_NORMAL: PASS (mean=0.00212, sd=1.02035)                           │
│     • TEST_EXPONENTIAL: PASS (mean=0.98425, sd=0.99914)                      │
│  • test() writes the sample files listed above and prints PASS/FAIL lines    │
│    with mean and sd as required.                                             │
│                                                                              │
│ Key findings / lessons learned                                               │
│                                                                              │
│  • ARS must explicitly handle constant (or nearly-constant) derivatives:     │
│    parallel tangents create undefined finite intersections. A robust ARS     │
│    implementation either special-cases purely linear log-densities (analytic │
│    sampling) or merges segments appropriately.                               │
│  • Numerical stability in intersections and derivative estimation is         │
│    crucial; small tolerances and uniqueness/sorting of abscissae are         │
│    necessary to avoid degeneracies.                                          │
│  • Clear validation and informative errors improve usability and make        │
│    diagnosing failures straightforward.                                      │
